Try per-entity first.\n    per_entity: Dict[str, float] = {}\n    default_value: Optional[float] = None\n\n    # If there's a row containing Meal_Kit_ID, treat it as a mapping table.\n    all_values_row1 = [ws.cell(1, c).value for c in range(1, ws.max_column + 1)]\n    if \"Meal_Kit_ID\" in all_values_row1 and \"Boxes_Per_Pallet\" in all_values_row1:\n        header_row, col = find_header_row(ws, [\"Meal_Kit_ID\", \"Boxes_Per_Pallet\"], scan_rows=50)\n        for r in range(header_row + 1, ws.max_row + 1):\n            mk = ws.cell(r, col[\"Meal_Kit_ID\"]).value\n            if mk is None or (isinstance(mk, str) and not mk.strip()):\n                continue\n            v = ws.cell(r, col[\"Boxes_Per_Pallet\"]).value\n            if v is None:\n                continue\n            per_entity[str(mk).strip()] = float(v)\n        return per_entity, None\n\n    #
